Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in symplectic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 578 and the invariant I(A,B) = 93 mod 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 578 + O(1/n^93). Summing over all modes and applying the symplectic convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^613(S, F) is trivial. QED.
